

ROC_remove_all_ideas = {
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_ideas = {
			ROC_Xu_Tingyao_idea
			ROC_Wang_Jingwei_idea
			ROC_Wang_Jingwei_idea2
			ROC_Wang_Jingwei_idea3
			ROC_Provisional_Senate_idea
			ROC_Song_Jiaoren_idea
			ROC_jiangjingguo_idea
			ROC_Chen_Qianyu_idea
			ROC_Perlica_idea
			ROC_songqingling_idea
			ROC_Rossi_idea
			ROC_Dai_Anlan_idea
			ROC_Hu_Zongnan_idea
			ROC_Tangtang_idea
			ROC_Sun_Ke_idea
			ROC_Dai_Jitao_idea
			ROC_Mi_Fu_idea
			ROC_zhou_enlai_idea
			ROC_Lidesheng_idea
			ROC_Chiyoda_Momo_idea
			ROC_Shu_idea
			ROC_Chen_Yun_idea
			ROC_Yoshida_Yuko_idea
			ROC_Xigua_idea
			ROC_Zhuang_Fangyi_idea
			ROC_Herrscher_of_Sentience_idea
			ROC_chencheng_idea
		}
		clear_variable = deputy_country_leader_var
	}
}

##对外关系部
ROC_foreign_influence_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ENG = {
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 0 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 2 } }
			}
		}
		THIS = { ROC_add_idea_ENG_very_low_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { ROC_ENG_cic_to_target_factor = 0.02 }
		set_variable = { ROC_ENG_buff_factor = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			ENG = {
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 2 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 4 } }
			}
		}
		THIS = { ROC_add_idea_ENG_low_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { ROC_ENG_cic_to_target_factor = 0.04 }
		set_variable = { ROC_ENG_buff_factor = 0.1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			ENG = {
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 4 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 6 } }
			}
		}
		THIS = { ROC_add_idea_ENG_middle_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { ROC_ENG_cic_to_target_factor = 0.06 }
		set_variable = { ROC_ENG_buff_factor = 0.15 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			ENG = {
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 6 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 8 } }
			}
		}
		THIS = { ROC_add_idea_ENG_high_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { ROC_ENG_cic_to_target_factor = 0.08 }
		set_variable = { ROC_ENG_buff_factor = 0.2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			ENG = {
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 8 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 10 } }
			}
		}
		THIS = { ROC_add_idea_ENG_very_high_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { ROC_ENG_cic_to_target_factor = 0.1 }
		set_variable = { ROC_ENG_buff_factor = 0.25 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			JAP = {
				is_in_array = { ROC.ROC_foreign_influence_array = THIS }
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 0 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 2 } }
			}
		}
		THIS = { ROC_add_idea_JAP_very_low_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { ROC_JAP_cic_to_target_factor = 0.02 }
		set_variable = { ROC_JAP_buff_factor = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			JAP = {
				is_in_array = { ROC.ROC_foreign_influence_array = THIS }
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 2 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 4 } }
			}
		}
		THIS = { ROC_add_idea_JAP_low_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { ROC_JAP_cic_to_target_factor = 0.04 }
		set_variable = { ROC_JAP_buff_factor = 0.1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			JAP = {
				is_in_array = { ROC.ROC_foreign_influence_array = THIS }
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 4 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 6 } }
			}
		}
		THIS = { ROC_add_idea_JAP_middle_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { ROC_JAP_cic_to_target_factor = 0.06 }
		set_variable = { ROC_JAP_buff_factor = 0.15 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			JAP = {
				is_in_array = { ROC.ROC_foreign_influence_array = THIS }
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 6 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 8 } }
			}
		}
		THIS = { ROC_add_idea_JAP_high_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { ROC_JAP_cic_to_target_factor = 0.08 }
		set_variable = { ROC_JAP_buff_factor = 0.2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			JAP = {
				is_in_array = { ROC.ROC_foreign_influence_array = THIS }
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 8 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 10 } }
			}
		}
		THIS = { ROC_add_idea_JAP_very_high_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { ROC_JAP_cic_to_target_factor = 0.1 }
		set_variable = { ROC_JAP_buff_factor = 0.25 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			SOV = {
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 0 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 2 } }
			}
		}
		THIS = { ROC_add_idea_SOV_very_low_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { ROC_SOV_cic_to_target_factor = 0.02 }
		set_variable = { ROC_SOV_buff_factor = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			SOV = {
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 2 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 4 } }
			}
		}
		THIS = { ROC_add_idea_SOV_low_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { ROC_SOV_cic_to_target_factor = 0.04 }
		set_variable = { ROC_SOV_buff_factor = 0.1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			SOV = {
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 4 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 6 } }
			}
		}

			}

		set_variable = { ROC_SOV_cic_to_target_factor = 0.08 }
		set_variable = { ROC_SOV_buff_factor = 0.2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			SOV = {
				check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 8 }
				not = { check_variable = { InfluenceWithROC > 10 } }
			}
		}
		THIS = { ROC_add_idea_SOV_very_high_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { ROC_SOV_cic_to_target_factor = 0.1 }
		set_variable = { ROC_SOV_buff_factor = 0.25 }

	if = {

 ##民族精神
	ROC_add_idea_ENG_very_high_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_high
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_high
				add_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_very_high
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_middle
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_middle
				add_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_very_high
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_low
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_low
				add_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_very_high
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_very_low
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_very_low
				add_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_very_high
			}
		}
	}
	ROC_add_idea_ENG_high_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_very_high
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_very_high
				add_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_high
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_middle
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_middle
				add_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_high
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_low
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_low
				add_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_high
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_very_low
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_very_low
				add_idea = ROC_influence_of_ENG_high
			}
		}
	}

	}

ROC_Democratic_elections_vice_president = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					ruling_only = yes
					character = ROC_Chiang_Kaishek
				}
			}
			ROC_remove_all_ideas = yes
			add_ideas = ROC_Chiang_Kaishek_idea2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					ruling_only = yes
					character = ROC_Wang_Jingwei
				}
			}
			ROC_remove_all_ideas = yes
			add_ideas = ROC_Wang_Jingwei_idea3
		}
	}
}

ROC_policy_window_button_open_effect = {  ##打开UI
	clear_array = ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array
	clear_array = ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array
	clear_array = ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array
	clear_array = ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array
	clear_array = ROC_policy_unlock_card_array
	clear_array = ROC_policy_unlock_card_array_temp
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array_true
		value = v
		break = break
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array_true
		value = v
		break = break
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array_true
		value = v
		break = break
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array_true
		value = v
		break = break
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_policy_unlock_card_array_true
		value = v
		break = break
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_unlock_card_array_temp = v }
	}
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			any_of = {
				array = ROC_policy_unlock_card_array_temp
				value = v
				index = i
				check_variable = { v < 999 }
			}
		}
		find_lowest_in_array = {
			array = ROC_policy_unlock_card_array_temp
			value = temp_lowest_v
			index = temp_i
		}
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_unlock_card_array = temp_lowest_v }
		set_variable = { ROC_policy_unlock_card_array_temp^temp_i = 999 }
	}
}

ROC_policy_window_button_close_effect = {  ##保存并关闭UI
	clear_array = ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array_true
	clear_array = ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array_true
	clear_array = ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array_true
	clear_array = ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array_true
	clear_array = ROC_policy_unlock_card_array_true
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array
		value = v
		break = break
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array_true = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array
		value = v
		break = break
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array_true = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array
		value = v
		break = break
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array_true = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array
		value = v
		break = break
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array_true = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_policy_unlock_card_array
		value = v
		break = break
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_unlock_card_array_true = v }
	}
	ROC_policy_card_modifier = yes
}
ROC_policy_card_modifier = {  ##添加buff
	hidden_effect = {
		set_variable = { UPSM_political_power_gain = 0 }
		for_each_loop = {
			array = ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array_true
			value = v
			break = break
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { v = 0 } }
				add_to_variable = { UPSM_political_power_gain = 0.2 }
			}
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array_true
			value = v
			break = break
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { v = 0 } }
				add_to_variable = { UPSM_political_power_gain = 0.2 }
			}
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array_true
			value = v
			break = break
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { v = 0 } }
				add_to_variable = { UPSM_political_power_gain = 0.2 }
			}
		}
		for_each_loop = {
			array = ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array_true
			value = v
			break = break
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { v = 0 } }
				add_to_variable = { UPSM_political_power_gain = 0.2 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				or = {
					is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array_true = 1 }

					is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array_true = 1 }

ROC_policy_delete_card = {  ##删除某个政策卡
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array_true = card_to_delete } }
		remove_from_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array_true = card_to_delete }
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array_true = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array_true = card_to_delete } }
		remove_from_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array_true = card_to_delete }
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array_true = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array_true = card_to_delete } }
		remove_from_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array_true = card_to_delete }
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array_true = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array_true = card_to_delete } }
		remove_from_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array_true = card_to_delete }
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array_true = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_unlock_card_array_true = card_to_delete } }
		remove_from_array = { ROC_policy_unlock_card_array_true = card_to_delete }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array = card_to_delete } }
		remove_from_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array = card_to_delete }
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array = card_to_delete } }
		remove_from_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array = card_to_delete }
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array = card_to_delete } }
		remove_from_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array = card_to_delete }
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array = card_to_delete } }
		remove_from_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array = card_to_delete }
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_unlock_card_array = card_to_delete } }
		remove_from_array = { ROC_policy_unlock_card_array = card_to_delete }
	}
}

ROC_policy_toast_refresh_frame = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_current < 31 } }
		set_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_frame = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_current < 61 } }
		set_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_frame = 3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_current < 91 } }
		set_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_frame = 4 }
	}
	else = { set_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_frame = 5 } }
	set_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_remaining = ROC_policy_toast_queue^num }
}

ROC_policy_toast_next_or_close = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_queue^num > 0 } }
		set_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_current = ROC_policy_toast_queue^0 }
		remove_from_array = { array = ROC_policy_toast_queue index = 0 }
		set_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_timer = 15 }
		ROC_policy_toast_refresh_frame = yes
	}
	else = {
		clear_variable = ROC_policy_toast_current
		clear_variable = ROC_policy_toast_frame
		clear_variable = ROC_policy_toast_timer
		clear_variable = ROC_policy_toast_remaining
	}
}

ROC_policy_unlock_card_with_toast = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_unlock_card_array_true = card_to_unlock } }
			NOT = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Military_array_true = card_to_unlock } }
			NOT = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array_true = card_to_unlock } }
			NOT = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Diplomacy_array_true = card_to_unlock } }
			NOT = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array_true = card_to_unlock } }
		}
		add_to_array = { ROC_policy_unlock_card_array_true = card_to_unlock }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ROC_policy_window_visible }
			add_to_array = { ROC_policy_unlock_card_array = card_to_unlock }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_current > 0 } } }
				set_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_current = card_to_unlock }
				set_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_timer = 15 }
			}
			else = { add_to_array = { ROC_policy_toast_queue = card_to_unlock } }
			ROC_policy_toast_refresh_frame = yes
		}
	}
}

ROC_check_generic_policy_card_unlocks = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ROC_gpcard_unlocked_4 }
			num_of_naval_factories > 4
		}
		set_country_flag = ROC_gpcard_unlocked_4
		set_temp_variable = { card_to_unlock = 4 }
		ROC_policy_unlock_card_with_toast = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ROC_gpcard_unlocked_25 }
			OR = { has_tech = tech_logistics_company has_tech = motorised_infantry }
		}
		set_country_flag = ROC_gpcard_unlocked_25
		set_temp_variable = { card_to_unlock = 25 }
		ROC_policy_unlock_card_with_toast = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ROC_gpcard_unlocked_26 }
			OR = { has_tech = artillery1 num_of_military_factories > 19 }
		}
		set_country_flag = ROC_gpcard_unlocked_26
		set_temp_variable = { card_to_unlock = 26 }
		ROC_policy_unlock_card_with_toast = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ROC_gpcard_unlocked_27 }
			OR = { has_tech = landing_craft has_tech = mtg_landing_craft num_of_naval_factories > 9 }
		}
		set_country_flag = ROC_gpcard_unlocked_27
		set_temp_variable = { card_to_unlock = 27 }
		ROC_policy_unlock_card_with_toast = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ROC_gpcard_unlocked_58 }
			OR = { has_global_flag = ROC_establishment_of_the_capital_at_nanjing AND = { has_war = no num_of_civilian_factories > 29 } }
		}
		set_country_flag = ROC_gpcard_unlocked_58
		set_temp_variable = { card_to_unlock = 58 }
		ROC_policy_unlock_card_with_toast = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ROC_gpcard_unlocked_59 }
			has_war = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = ROC_gpcard_unlocked_59
		set_temp_variable = { card_to_unlock = 59 }

	}

ROC_policy_toast_daily_tick = {
	ROC_check_generic_policy_card_unlocks = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_current > 0 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_timer = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_policy_toast_timer > 0 } } }
			ROC_policy_toast_next_or_close = yes
		}
	}
}

ROC_factional_influence_calculation = {  ##计算广府派系影响力
	set_variable = { ROC_total_influence_var = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { KMT_main_party_array = 2 } }
		add_to_variable = { ROC_total_influence_var = kmt_party_percent^2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { KMT_main_party_array = 3 } }
		add_to_variable = { ROC_total_influence_var = kmt_party_percent^3 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { KMT_main_party_array = 4 } }
		add_to_variable = { ROC_total_influence_var = kmt_party_percent^4 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { KMT_main_party_array = 5 } }
		add_to_variable = { ROC_total_influence_var = kmt_party_percent^5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { KMT_main_party_array = 6 } }
		add_to_variable = { ROC_total_influence_var = kmt_party_percent^6 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { KMT_main_party_array = 7 } }
		add_to_variable = { ROC_total_influence_var = kmt_party_percent^7 }
	}
	divide_variable = { kmt_party_percent^2 = ROC_total_influence_var }
	divide_variable = { kmt_party_percent^3 = ROC_total_influence_var }
	divide_variable = { kmt_party_percent^4 = ROC_total_influence_var }
	divide_variable = { kmt_party_percent^5 = ROC_total_influence_var }
	divide_variable = { kmt_party_percent^6 = ROC_total_influence_var }
	divide_variable = { kmt_party_percent^7 = ROC_total_influence_var }
	clear_array = kmt_party_percent_true
	set_variable = { kmt_party_percent_true^2 = kmt_party_percent^2 }  ##民革
	set_variable = { kmt_party_percent_true^3 = kmt_party_percent^3 }  ##宪政会
	set_variable = { kmt_party_percent_true^4 = kmt_party_percent^4 }  ##CC
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { kmt_party_overlord^5 = 2 } }  ##中正
		add_to_variable = { kmt_party_percent_true^2 = kmt_party_percent^5 }
		set_variable = { kmt_party_percent_true^5 = 0 }
	}
	else = { set_variable = { kmt_party_percent_true^5 = kmt_party_percent^5 } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { kmt_party_overlord^6 = 2 } }  ##蓝衣社
		add_to_variable = { kmt_party_percent_true^2 = kmt_party_percent^6 }
	}
	else = { add_to_variable = { kmt_party_percent_true^5 = kmt_party_percent^6 } }
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { KMT_main_party_array = 7 } }
		set_variable = { kmt_party_percent_true^7 = kmt_party_percent^7 }  ##改组
	}
	else = { set_variable = { kmt_party_percent_true^7 = 0 } }
}
ROC_factional_influence_calculation_monthly = {  ##计算广府派系影响力（每月）
	add_to_variable = { kmt_party_percent^2 = modifier@ROC_RCKMT_influence_monthly }  ##民革
	add_to_variable = { kmt_party_percent^3 = modifier@ROC_Constitutionalists_influence_monthly }  ##民宪会
	add_to_variable = { kmt_party_percent^4 = modifier@ROC_CC_influence_monthly }  ##CC
	add_to_variable = { kmt_party_percent^5 = modifier@ROC_Whampoa_influence_monthly }  ##中正社
	add_to_variable = { kmt_party_percent^6 = modifier@ROC_CNRS_influence_monthly }  ##蓝衣社
	#add_to_variable = { kmt_party_percent^7 = modifier@ROC_RCAK_influence_monthly }  ##改组派
}

ROC_if_not_have_give_EM_modifier_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_dynamic_modifier = {
					modifier = ROC_economic_miracle_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = ROC_economic_miracle_modifier
		}
	}
}

###  上海合作组织计算  ###
Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization_effect = {
	###  统计经济阵营成员数量
	clear_array = global.Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization_member  ##成员所在的数组
	set_variable = { global.num_of_Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization = 0 }  ##成员数量变量
	every_country = {
		limit = { check_variable = { THIS.economy_faction_var = 13 } }
		add_to_array = { global.Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization_member = THIS }
		add_to_variable = { global.num_of_Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization = 1 }
	}
	###  计算动态阵营buff（如果有）
	Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization_leader_modifier_effect = yes
	###  刷新经济阵营GDP
	compute_global_gdp = yes
}

Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization_leader_modifier_effect = {
	set_variable = { global.Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization_leader_research_speed_factor = global.num_of_Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization }
	divide_variable = { global.Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization_leader_research_speed_factor = 200 }
	set_variable = { global.Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization_leader_global_building_slots_factor = global.num_of_Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization }
	divide_variable = { global.Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization_leader_global_building_slots_factor = 100 }
	set_variable = { global.Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization_leader_production_speed_buildings_factor = global.num_of_Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization }
	divide_variable = { global.Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization_leader_production_speed_buildings_factor = 200 }
}

###  饼图  ###

#计算执政联盟支持率
ROC_Calculate_support_of_Ruling_Coalition_Vate = {
	set_variable = { ROOT.Total_Influence_of_Ruling_Coalition = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { Ruling_coalition_array = 1 } }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.Total_Influence_of_Ruling_Coalition = ROOT.support_of_CCP }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { Ruling_coalition_array = 2 } }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.Total_Influence_of_Ruling_Coalition = ROOT.support_of_PWDP }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { Ruling_coalition_array = 3 } }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.Total_Influence_of_Ruling_Coalition = ROOT.support_of_KMT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { Ruling_coalition_array = 4 } }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.Total_Influence_of_Ruling_Coalition = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { Ruling_coalition_array = 5 } }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.Total_Influence_of_Ruling_Coalition = ROOT.support_of_ZhiGong }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { Ruling_coalition_array = 6 } }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.Total_Influence_of_Ruling_Coalition = ROOT.support_of_CLU }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { Ruling_coalition_array = 7 } }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.Total_Influence_of_Ruling_Coalition = ROOT.support_of_Youth }
	}
}
ROC_change_to_hoi_pie_chart = {
	hidden_effect = {
		clamp_variable = {
			var = support_of_CCP
			min = 0
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = support_of_PWDP
			min = 0
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = support_of_KMT
			min = 0
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = support_of_ZhiGong
			min = 0
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = support_of_CLU
			min = 0
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = support_of_Youth
			min = 0
		}
		set_variable = { ROOT.TOTAL_party_support = 0 }
		#计算百分比
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.TOTAL_party_support = ROOT.support_of_CCP }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.TOTAL_party_support = ROOT.support_of_PWDP }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.TOTAL_party_support = ROOT.support_of_KMT }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.TOTAL_party_support = ROOT.support_of_ZhiGong }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.TOTAL_party_support = ROOT.support_of_CLU }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.TOTAL_party_support = ROOT.support_of_Youth }
		divide_variable = { ROOT.support_of_CCP = ROOT.TOTAL_party_support }
		multiply_variable = { ROOT.support_of_CCP = 100 }
		divide_variable = { ROOT.support_of_PWDP = ROOT.TOTAL_party_support }
		multiply_variable = { ROOT.support_of_PWDP = 100 }
		divide_variable = { ROOT.support_of_KMT = ROOT.TOTAL_party_support }
		multiply_variable = { ROOT.support_of_KMT = 100 }
		divide_variable = { ROOT.support_of_ZhiGong = ROOT.TOTAL_party_support }
		multiply_variable = { ROOT.support_of_ZhiGong = 100 }
		divide_variable = { ROOT.support_of_CLU = ROOT.TOTAL_party_support }
		multiply_variable = { ROOT.support_of_CLU = 100 }
		divide_variable = { ROOT.support_of_Youth = ROOT.TOTAL_party_support }
		multiply_variable = { ROOT.support_of_Youth = 100 }
		#饼图
		set_variable = { ROOT.support_of_CCP_pie_chart_var = support_of_CCP }
		set_variable = { ROOT.support_of_PWDP_pie_chart_var = support_of_PWDP }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.support_of_PWDP_pie_chart_var = ROOT.support_of_CCP_pie_chart_var }
		set_variable = { ROOT.support_of_KMT_pie_chart_var = support_of_KMT }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.support_of_KMT_pie_chart_var = ROOT.support_of_PWDP_pie_chart_var }
		set_variable = { ROOT.support_of_ZhiGong_pie_chart_var = support_of_ZhiGong }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.support_of_ZhiGong_pie_chart_var = ROOT.support_of_KMT_pie_chart_var }
		set_variable = { ROOT.support_of_CLU_pie_chart_var = support_of_CLU }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.support_of_CLU_pie_chart_var = ROOT.support_of_ZhiGong_pie_chart_var }
		set_variable = { ROOT.support_of_Youth_pie_chart_var = support_of_Youth }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.support_of_Youth_pie_chart_var = ROOT.support_of_CLU_pie_chart_var }
		#康米
		set_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_communism = support_of_CCP }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_communism = support_of_CCP }
		set_variable = { ROOT.support_of_PWDP_left = support_of_PWDP }
		multiply_variable = { ROOT.support_of_PWDP_left = 0.5 }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_communism = support_of_PWDP_left }
		#民主
		set_variable = { ROOT.support_of_PWDP_right = support_of_PWDP }
		subtract_from_variable = { ROOT.support_of_PWDP_right = support_of_PWDP_left }
		set_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_democratic = support_of_PWDP_right }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_democratic = support_of_KMT }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_democratic = support_of_ZhiGong }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_democratic = support_of_CLU }
		#法西斯
		set_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_fascism = support_of_Youth }
		#中立
		set_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_neutrality = 0 }
		#
		set_variable = { ROOT.total_temp_popularity = temp_popularity_of_communism }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.total_temp_popularity = temp_popularity_of_democratic }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.total_temp_popularity = temp_popularity_of_fascism }
		add_to_variable = { ROOT.total_temp_popularity = temp_popularity_of_neutrality }
		divide_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_communism = ROOT.total_temp_popularity }
		multiply_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_communism = 100 }
		divide_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_democratic = ROOT.total_temp_popularity }
		multiply_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_democratic = 100 }
		divide_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_fascism = ROOT.total_temp_popularity }
		multiply_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_fascism = 100 }
		set_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_neutrality = 100 }
		subtract_from_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_neutrality = temp_popularity_of_communism }
		subtract_from_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_neutrality = temp_popularity_of_democratic }
		subtract_from_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_neutrality = temp_popularity_of_fascism }
		# divide_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_neutrality = ROOT.total_temp_popularity }
		# multiply_variable = { ROOT.temp_popularity_of_neutrality = 100 }
		set_popularities = {
			democratic = temp_popularity_of_democratic
			neutrality = temp_popularity_of_neutrality
			fascism = temp_popularity_of_fascism
			communism = temp_popularity_of_communism
		}
		ROC_Calculate_support_of_Ruling_Coalition_Vate = yes
		ROC_Counting_of_seats_in_National_Assembly = yes
	}
}
#国民议会席位计算
ROC_Counting_of_seats_in_National_Assembly = {
	set_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_CCP = support_of_CCP }
	divide_variable = { ROOT.ROCNA_seat_of_CCP = 100 }
	multiply_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_CCP = 577 }
	round_variable = ROCNA_seat_of_CCP
	set_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_PWDP = support_of_PWDP }
	divide_variable = { ROOT.ROCNA_seat_of_PWDP = 100 }
	multiply_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_PWDP = 577 }
	round_variable = ROCNA_seat_of_PWDP
	set_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_KMT = support_of_KMT }
	divide_variable = { ROOT.ROCNA_seat_of_KMT = 100 }
	multiply_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_KMT = 577 }
	round_variable = ROCNA_seat_of_KMT
	set_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_ZhiGong = support_of_ZhiGong }
	divide_variable = { ROOT.ROCNA_seat_of_ZhiGong = 100 }
	multiply_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_ZhiGong = 577 }
	round_variable = ROCNA_seat_of_ZhiGong
	set_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_CLU = support_of_CLU }
	divide_variable = { ROOT.ROCNA_seat_of_CLU = 100 }
	multiply_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_CLU = 577 }
	round_variable = ROCNA_seat_of_CLU
	set_variable = { ROOT.ROCNA_seat_of_Youth = 577 }
	subtract_from_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_Youth = ROCNA_seat_of_CCP }
	subtract_from_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_Youth = ROCNA_seat_of_PWDP }
	subtract_from_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_Youth = ROCNA_seat_of_KMT }
	subtract_from_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_Youth = ROCNA_seat_of_ZhiGong }
	subtract_from_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_Youth = ROCNA_seat_of_CLU }
	set_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_DPA = ROCNA_seat_of_PWDP }
	add_to_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_DPA = ROCNA_seat_of_ZhiGong }
	add_to_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_DPA = ROCNA_seat_of_CLU }
	add_to_variable = { ROCNA_seat_of_DPA = ROCNA_seat_of_Youth }

	set_variable = { ROOT.ROCNA_seatend_of_CCP = ROCNA_seat_of_CCP }
	set_variable = { ROOT.ROCNA_seatend_of_PWDP = ROCNA_seat_of_PWDP }
	set_variable = { ROOT.ROCNA_seatend_of_KMT = ROCNA_seat_of_KMT }
	set_variable = { ROOT.ROCNA_seatend_of_ZhiGong = ROCNA_seat_of_ZhiGong }
	set_variable = { ROOT.ROCNA_seatend_of_CLU = ROCNA_seat_of_CLU }
	set_variable = { ROOT.ROCNA_seatend_of_Youth = ROCNA_seat_of_Youth }
	#变色
	clear_array = ROC_National_Assembly_Array
	resize_array = {
		array = ROC_National_Assembly_Array
		value = 1
		size = ROCNA_seatend_of_CCP
	}
	add_to_variable = { ROCNA_seatend_of_PWDP = ROCNA_seatend_of_CCP  }
	resize_array = {
		array = ROC_National_Assembly_Array
		value = 2
		size = ROCNA_seatend_of_PWDP
	}
	add_to_variable = { ROCNA_seatend_of_KMT = ROCNA_seatend_of_PWDP  }
	resize_array = {
		array = ROC_National_Assembly_Array
		value = 3
		size = ROCNA_seatend_of_KMT
	}
	add_to_variable = { ROCNA_seatend_of_ZhiGong = ROCNA_seatend_of_KMT  }
	resize_array = {
		array = ROC_National_Assembly_Array
		value = 5
		size = ROCNA_seatend_of_ZhiGong
	}
	add_to_variable = { ROCNA_seatend_of_CLU = ROCNA_seatend_of_ZhiGong  }
	resize_array = {
		array = ROC_National_Assembly_Array
		value = 6
		size = ROCNA_seatend_of_CLU
	}
	add_to_variable = { ROCNA_seatend_of_Youth = ROCNA_seatend_of_CLU  }
	resize_array = {
		array = ROC_National_Assembly_Array
		value = 7
		size = ROCNA_seatend_of_Youth
	}
}

	}

	}

	}

ROC_PCC_event_modifier_effect = {
	#蓝过低
	if = {
		limit = {
			democratic < 0.1
		}
		country_event = roc.181
	}
	#棕过低
	if = {
		limit = {
			fascism < 0.02
		}
		country_event = roc.182
	}
	#灰过低
	if = {
		limit = {
			neutrality < 0.05
		}
		country_event = roc.183
	}
	#红过低
	if = {
		limit = {
			communism < 0.6
		}
		country_event = roc.184
	}
	#红过高
	if = {
		limit = {
			communism > 0.8
		}
		country_event = roc.327
	}
	
}

ROC_collection_passive_idea_check = {
	## 前30个藏品的被动精神同步：只要拥有（未激活/已激活数组里都有）却没有对应 idea，就补挂
	## 1 母鼎
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { KMT_collection_unusing_array = 1 }
				is_in_array = { KMT_collection_using_array = 1 }
			}
			NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_1_passive_idea }
		}
		add_ideas = ROC_collection_1_passive_idea
	}
	## 2 嫩竹
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { KMT_collection_unusing_array = 2 }
				is_in_array = { KMT_collection_using_array = 2 }
			}
			NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_2_passive_idea }
		}
		add_ideas = ROC_collection_2_passive_idea
	}
	## 4 伥物房账簿

ROC_collection_gain_effect = {
	## 藏品/钱币「获得时立刻生效」的效果派发器
	## 调用前先把获得的 id 写进临时变量 ROC_collection_new_id_var，执行完自动清空
	## 3 古旧铸物：打开将领选择界面（ROC_unit_leader_level_up_window），由玩家指定3名陆军将领升级
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_new_id_var = 3 } }
		set_variable = { num_unit_leader_level_up = 3 }
	}
	## 14 烟烛：全体海军将领升1级
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_new_id_var = 14 } }
		every_navy_leader = {
			add_skill_level = 1
		}
	}
	## 15 三足金雏：获得100pp、200hcp
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_new_id_var = 15 } }
		add_political_power = 100
		add_to_variable = { ubs_save = 200 }
	}
	## 26 古旧钱币：获得200hcp
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_new_id_var = 26 } }
		add_to_variable = { ubs_save = 200 }
	}
	## 30 热水壶：获得50pp、50hcp
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_new_id_var = 30 } }
		add_political_power = 50
		add_to_variable = { ubs_save = 50 }
	}
	clear_variable = ROC_collection_new_id_var
}

ROC_collection_gain_random_one = {
	## 随机获得一个当前尚未拥有的藏品（1-30）
	## 一个都没得给时兜底发热水壶（30）；热水壶可重复持有，已有时改为数量+1
	set_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_gain_random_ok = 0 }
	random_list = {
		100 = {
			modifier = {
				or = {
					is_in_array = { KMT_collection_using_array = 1 }
					is_in_array = { KMT_collection_unusing_array = 1 }
					is_in_array = { KMT_spnode_shop_goods_array = 1 }
					is_in_array = { KMT_sp_shop_goods_array = 1 }
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			add_to_array = { KMT_collection_unusing_array = 1 }
			set_variable = { ROC_collection_random_last_id = 1 }
			set_variable = { ROC_collection_new_id_var = 1 }
			ROC_collection_gain_effect = yes
			ROC_collection_passive_idea_check = yes
			set_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_gain_random_ok = 1 }
		}
		100 = {
			modifier = {
				or = {
					is_in_array = { KMT_collection_using_array = 2 }
					is_in_array = { KMT_collection_unusing_array = 2 }

ROC_collection_39_leave_effect = {
	## 39 花-烽火台：离开钱盒时的奖励
	add_political_power = 200
	add_to_variable = { ubs_save = 400 }
}

ROC_collection_12_bonus_calc = {
	## 12 契心聆铃：按累计王牌飞行员数算加成（上限10）
	set_variable = { ROC_collection_12_passive_bonus_factor = ROC_ace_promoted_num }
	clamp_variable = { var = ROC_collection_12_passive_bonus_factor max = 10 }
	multiply_variable = { ROC_collection_12_passive_bonus_factor = 0.05 }
	set_variable = { ROC_collection_12_active_bonus_factor = ROC_ace_promoted_num }
	clamp_variable = { var = ROC_collection_12_active_bonus_factor max = 10 }
	multiply_variable = { ROC_collection_12_active_bonus_factor = 0.02 }
}

ROC_collection_8_bonus_calc = {
	## 8 拳经三问：基础5%陆军攻击，每有一个集团军指挥部额外+2%（上限5个）
	set_variable = { ROC_collection_8_bonus_factor = 0.05 }
	set_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c8_num = 0 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c8_num = num_armies_with_type@hq_infantry }
	add_to_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c8_num = num_armies_with_type@hq_motorized }
	add_to_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c8_num = num_armies_with_type@hq_armored_car }
	add_to_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c8_num = num_armies_with_type@hq_paratrooper }
	add_to_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c8_num = num_armies_with_type@hq_light_armor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c8_num = num_armies_with_type@hq_medium_armor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c8_num = num_armies_with_type@hq_heavy_armor }
	clamp_variable = { var = temp_ROC_c8_num max = 5 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c8_num = 0.02 }
	add_to_variable = { ROC_collection_8_bonus_factor = temp_ROC_c8_num }
}

ROC_coin_box_hua_coin_calc = {
	## 统计钱盒里的花钱数量（38-43），并刷新 5 号酿山河的加成
	set_variable = { ROC_hua_coin_in_box_num = 0 }
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_collection_coin_box
		value = v
		index = i
		break = break
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { v > 37 }
				not = { check_variable = { v > 43 } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { ROC_hua_coin_in_box_num = 1 }
		}
	}
	## 5 号酿山河：每有一枚花钱，海军组织 / 海军攻击 +2.5%
	set_variable = { ROC_collection_5_passive_bonus_factor = ROC_hua_coin_in_box_num }
	multiply_variable = { ROC_collection_5_passive_bonus_factor = 0.025 }
	set_variable = { ROC_collection_5_active_bonus_factor = ROC_hua_coin_in_box_num }
	multiply_variable = { ROC_collection_5_active_bonus_factor = 0.025 }
}

ROC_coin_flipped_hua_coin_calc = {
	## 统计本轮投出的花钱数量（38-43），供 6 号吉运有三判定
	set_variable = { ROC_hua_coin_flipped_num = 0 }
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_coin_flipped_id_array
		value = v
		index = i
		break = break
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { v > 37 }
				not = { check_variable = { v > 43 } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { ROC_hua_coin_flipped_num = 1 }
		}
	}
}

ROC_coin_flipped_idea_effect = {
	## 投出时的一次性效果：遍历本轮投出的钱币，直接结算（不走 idea 的 on_add）
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_coin_flipped_id_array
		value = v
		index = i
		break = break
		## 31 衡-奇土生金：+400hcp
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { v = 31 } }
			add_to_variable = { ubs_save = 400 }
		}
		## 32 衡-金寒水衍：+200hcp
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { v = 32 } }
			add_to_variable = { ubs_save = 200 }
		}
		## 38 花-火灼土沃：随机地块基础设施等级+1，并弹窗提示
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { v = 38 } }
			ROOT = {
				random_owned_state = {
					limit = { is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT }
					set_variable = { ROOT.ROC_collection_38_state = THIS.id }
					add_building_construction = {
						type = infrastructure
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
				## 弹窗（编号5：基础设施提升）
				add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 5 }
				add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
				add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
				add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
				add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
				add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
				adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operation = yes
			}
		}
		## 40 花-茧成绢：每4点hcp换1点hcp
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { v = 40 } }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_coin40_hcp = ubs_save }
			divide_variable = { temp_ROC_coin40_hcp = 4 }
			round_variable = temp_ROC_coin40_hcp
			add_to_variable = { ubs_save = temp_ROC_coin40_hcp }
		}
		## 41 花-大荒盛景：随机获得2个藏品，并弹窗提示
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { v = 41 } }
			clear_variable = ROC_random_gained_1
			clear_variable = ROC_random_gained_2
			ROC_collection_gain_random_one = yes
			set_variable = { ROC_random_gained_1 = ROC_collection_random_last_id }
			ROC_collection_gain_random_one = yes
			set_variable = { ROC_random_gained_2 = ROC_collection_random_last_id }
			## 弹窗（编号6：大荒盛景）
			add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 6 }
			add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
			add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
			add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
			add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
			add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
			adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operation = yes
		}
		## 46 厉-合乎礼：每次投出失去100hcp
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { v = 46 } }
			add_to_variable = { ubs_save = -100 }
		}
		## 33 衡-志欲遂：累计投出次数 + 挂修正器
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { v = 33 } }
			add_to_variable = { num_coin_tossed_33 = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_33_modifier } } }
					add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_33_modifier }
				}
			}
		}
		## 42 花-百业俱兴：累计投出次数 + 挂修正器
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { v = 42 } }
			add_to_variable = { num_coin_tossed_42 = 1 }
			ROOT = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_42_modifier } } }
					add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_42_modifier }
				}
			}
		}
		## 投出类精神：投出即补挂（available 负责不投出时自动失效）
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { v = 34 }
				NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_34_idea }
			}
			add_ideas = ROC_collection_34_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { v = 35 }
				NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_35_idea }
			}
			add_ideas = ROC_collection_35_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { v = 43 }
				NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_43_idea }
			}
			add_ideas = ROC_collection_43_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { v = 44 }
				NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_44_idea }
			}
			add_ideas = ROC_collection_44_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { v = 45 }
				NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_45_idea }
			}
			add_ideas = ROC_collection_45_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { v = 47 }
				NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_47_idea }
			}
			add_ideas = ROC_collection_47_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { v = 48 }
				NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_48_idea }
			}
			add_ideas = ROC_collection_48_idea
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { v = 49 }
				NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_49_idea }
			}
			add_ideas = ROC_collection_49_idea
		}

	}
	## 统计本轮投出的花钱数量
	ROC_coin_flipped_hua_coin_calc = yes
	## 6 吉运有三：需先拥有该藏品；主动激活期间投出1枚即可，平时需投出3枚
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { KMT_collection_unusing_array = 6 }
				is_in_array = { KMT_collection_using_array = 6 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { KMT_collection_using_array = 6 } }
				check_variable = { ROC_hua_coin_flipped_num > 0 }
			}
			else = { check_variable = { ROC_hua_coin_flipped_num > 2 } }
		}
		add_to_variable = { ubs_save = 500 }
		add_political_power = 200
	}
	## 刷新 33 / 42 的加成数值
	ROC_collection_coin_bonus_calc = yes
}

ROC_collection_active_bonus_calc = {
	## 藏品主动效果的数值计算（依赖重复购买次数 ROC_collection_using_num^N）
	## 1 母鼎：基础 5%，每额外购买一次 +2%，额外最多算 5 次
	set_variable = { ROC_collection_1_bonus_factor = 0.05 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_using_num^1 > 1 } }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c1_extra = ROC_collection_using_num^1 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c1_extra = -1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = temp_ROC_c1_extra max = 5 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c1_extra = 0.02 }
		add_to_variable = { ROC_collection_1_bonus_factor = temp_ROC_c1_extra }
	}
	## 2 嫩竹：基础 5%，每额外购买一次 +3%，额外最多算 5 次
	set_variable = { ROC_collection_2_bonus_factor = 0.05 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_using_num^2 > 1 } }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c2_extra = ROC_collection_using_num^2 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c2_extra = -1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = temp_ROC_c2_extra max = 5 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c2_extra = 0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { ROC_collection_2_bonus_factor = temp_ROC_c2_extra }
	}
}

ROC_collection_coin_bonus_calc = {
	## 钱币投出效果的数值计算（依赖累计投出次数）
	## 33 志欲遂：+40%，每额外投出一次 -10%，最低 0
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { num_coin_tossed_33 > 0 } }
		set_variable = { ROC_collection_33_bonus_factor = num_coin_tossed_33 }
		add_to_variable = { ROC_collection_33_bonus_factor = -1 }
		multiply_variable = { ROC_collection_33_bonus_factor = -0.1 }
		add_to_variable = { ROC_collection_33_bonus_factor = 0.4 }
		clamp_variable = { var = ROC_collection_33_bonus_factor min = 0 max = 0.4 }
	}
	else = { set_variable = { ROC_collection_33_bonus_factor = 0 } }
	## 42 百业俱兴：每次投出 +5%，最多累计 3 次
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { num_coin_tossed_42 > 0 } }
		set_variable = { ROC_collection_42_bonus_factor = num_coin_tossed_42 }
		clamp_variable = { var = ROC_collection_42_bonus_factor max = 3 }
		multiply_variable = { ROC_collection_42_bonus_factor = 0.05 }
	}
	else = { set_variable = { ROC_collection_42_bonus_factor = 0 } }
}

ROC_collection_proactive_effect = {
	## 藏品「主动激活」瞬间生效的效果派发器
	## 调用前先把激活的藏品 id 写进临时变量 ROC_collection_active_id_var，执行完自动清空
	## 3 古旧铸物：打开将领选择界面（ROC_unit_leader_level_up_window），由玩家指定1名陆军将领升级
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 3 } }
		set_variable = { num_unit_leader_level_up = 1 }
	}
	## 激活类精神：激活即补挂（available 负责不激活时自动失效）
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 4 }
			NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_4_proactive_idea }
		}
		add_ideas = ROC_collection_4_proactive_idea
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 8 }
			NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_8_proactive_idea }
		}
		add_ideas = ROC_collection_8_proactive_idea
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 9 }
			NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_9_proactive_idea }
		}
		add_ideas = ROC_collection_9_proactive_idea
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 10 }
			NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_10_proactive_idea }
		}
		add_ideas = ROC_collection_10_proactive_idea
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 13 }
			NOT = { has_idea = ROC_collection_13_proactive_idea }
		}
		add_ideas = ROC_collection_13_proactive_idea
	}

	## 1 母鼎：陆军攻击（基础5% + 每次额外购买2%，上限5次）
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 1 } }
		ROC_collection_active_bonus_calc = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_1_modifier } } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_1_modifier }
		}
	}
	## 2 嫩竹：陆军防御（基础5% + 每次额外购买3%，上限5次）
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 2 } }
		ROC_collection_active_bonus_calc = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_2_modifier } } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_2_modifier }
		}
	}
	## 5 酿山河：主动（钱盒每枚花钱 → 海军攻击），修正器自带 remove_trigger
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 5 } }
		ROC_coin_box_hua_coin_calc = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_5_active_modifier } } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_5_active_modifier }
		}
	}
	## 12 契心聆铃：主动（王牌飞行员数 → 空军机动）
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 12 } }
		ROC_collection_12_bonus_calc = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_12_active_modifier } } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_12_active_modifier }
		}
	}
	## 14 烟烛：令随机一名海军将领获得「海军血统」，或随机一名陆军将领获得「职业军官」
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 14 } }
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				random_navy_leader = {
					add_trait = { trait = naval_lineage }
					character = { save_global_event_target_as = ROC_collection_14_leader }
				}
				## 弹窗（编号3：海军血统）——用 ROOT 切回本国作用域
				ROOT = {
					add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 3 }
					add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
					add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
					add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
					add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
					add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
					adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operation = yes
				}
			}
			50 = {
				random_army_leader = {
					add_trait = { trait = career_officer }
					character = { save_global_event_target_as = ROC_collection_14_leader }
				}
				## 弹窗（编号4：职业军官）——用 ROOT 切回本国作用域
				ROOT = {
					add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 4 }
					add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
					add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
					add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
					add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
					add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
					adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operation = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	## 15 三足金雏：令pp、hcp中任意一项翻倍
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 15 } }
		country_event = {  ##三足金雏
			id = roc.397
		}
	}
	## 7 「福祸相依」：立即获得1枚随机厉钱
	## 只从「钱盒里没有、两个商店也没有」的厉钱里抽；一个都没有时随机不生效
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 7 } }
		random_list = {
			100 = {

					or = {
						is_in_array = { ROC_collection_coin_box = 45 }
						is_in_array = { KMT_spnode_shop_goods_array = 45 }
						is_in_array = { KMT_sp_shop_goods_array = 45 }
					}
					factor = 0
				}
				set_variable = { ROC_coin_new_var = 45 }
			}
			100 = {
				modifier = {
					or = {
						is_in_array = { ROC_collection_coin_box = 46 }
						is_in_array = { KMT_spnode_shop_goods_array = 46 }
						is_in_array = { KMT_sp_shop_goods_array = 46 }

						is_in_array = { KMT_sp_shop_goods_array = 47 }
					}
					factor = 0

				set_variable = { ROC_coin_new_var = 47 }
			}
			100 = {

						is_in_array = { KMT_sp_shop_goods_array = 49 }
					}
					factor = 0
				}
				set_variable = { ROC_coin_new_var = 49 }
			}
		}
	}
	## 11 错易锁：随机4个己方地块获得 +25% 地块建造速度，并弹窗提示
	## 修正器 ROC_collection_11_modifier 挂在州上，自带 remove_trigger，脱离激活数组后自动移除
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_active_id_var = 11 } }
		ROOT = {
			set_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c11_i = 0 }
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = { is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT }
				random_select_amount = 4
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_11_modifier } } }
					add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_collection_11_modifier }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { check_variable = { temp_ROC_c11_i = 0 } } }
					set_variable = { ROOT.ROC_collection_11_state1 = THIS.id }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { check_variable = { temp_ROC_c11_i = 1 } } }
					set_variable = { ROOT.ROC_collection_11_state2 = THIS.id }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { check_variable = { temp_ROC_c11_i = 2 } } }
					set_variable = { ROOT.ROC_collection_11_state3 = THIS.id }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { check_variable = { temp_ROC_c11_i = 3 } } }
					set_variable = { ROOT.ROC_collection_11_state4 = THIS.id }
				}
				ROOT = { add_to_temp_variable = { temp_ROC_c11_i = 1 } }
			}
			## 弹窗（编号2：地块建设加速）
			add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 2 }
			add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }

			add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
			add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
			add_to_array = { adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operations = 0 }
			adorable_heart_prompt_alert_operation = yes
		}
	}
	clear_variable = ROC_collection_active_id_var
}

ROC_Collection_daily = {
	## 刷新钱盒花钱统计（5 号）与 8 号加成
	ROC_coin_box_hua_coin_calc = yes
	## 刷新 8 号（集团军指挥部数量）的加成
	ROC_collection_8_bonus_calc = yes
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_collection_remaining_cooling_days  ##冷却剩余天数
		value = v
		index = i
		break = break
		if = {  ##对数组中每个元素执行效果
			limit = {
				not = { check_variable = { v = 0 } }
				check_variable = { i < 16 }
				check_variable = { i > 0 }
				is_in_array = { KMT_collection_unusing_array = i }
			}
			add_to_variable = { ROC_collection_remaining_cooling_days^i = -1 }
		}
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_collection_remaining_effect_days  ##效果剩余天数
		value = v
		index = i
		break = break
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { i < 16 }
				check_variable = { i > 0 }
				is_in_array = { KMT_collection_using_array = i }
			}
			if = {  ##对数组中每个元素执行效果
				limit = {
					check_variable = { v = 0 }
				}
				add_to_array = { KMT_collection_unusing_array = i }
				remove_from_array = { KMT_collection_using_array = i }
				##这里写效果触发器
				set_variable = { ROC_collection_remaining_cooling_days^i = ROC_collection_cooling_days^i }
				add_to_variable = { num_of_activated_collection = -1 }
			}
			else = { add_to_variable = { ROC_collection_remaining_effect_days^i = -1 } }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_auto_countdown_days = 0 } }
		ROC_coin_flipped_refresh = yes
	}
	else = { add_to_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_auto_countdown_days = -1 } }
}

	ROC_Collection_shop_refresh_effect = yes
}

		array = KMT_sp_shop_goods_array  ##行商
		value = 0
		size = 7
	}
	ROC_Collection_sp_shop_refresh_effect = yes
}

ROC_coin_flipped_refresh = {
	## 变换：只有「上一轮实际投出」的 31 奇土生金 / 32 金寒水衍 / 41 大荒盛景，
	## 才会在下次投钱前变为 37 大炎通宝（钱盒里有但没投出的保持不变）
	for_each_loop = {
		array = ROC_collection_coin_box
		value = v
		index = i
		break = break
		if = {
			limit = {
				or = {
					and = {
						check_variable = { v = 31 }
						is_in_array = { ROC_coin_flipped_id_array = 31 }
					}
					and = {
						check_variable = { v = 32 }
						is_in_array = { ROC_coin_flipped_id_array = 32 }
					}
					and = {
						check_variable = { v = 41 }
						is_in_array = { ROC_coin_flipped_id_array = 41 }
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^i = 37 }
		}
	}
	random_list = {
		100 = {
			modifier = {
				or = {
					check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_1_var = 1 }
					check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_2_var = 1 }
					check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_3_var = 1 }
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				or = {
					check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^1 = 36 }
					check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^1 = 39 }
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			set_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_1_var = 1 }
		}
		100 = {
			modifier = {
				or = {
					check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_1_var = 2 }
					check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_2_var = 2 }
					check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_3_var = 2 }
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				or = {
					check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^2 = 36 }
					check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^2 = 39 }
				}
				factor = 0

				}
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				or = {
					check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^5 = 36 }
					check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^5 = 39 }
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			set_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_3_var = 5 }
		}
		100 = {
			modifier = {
				or = {
					check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_1_var = 6 }
					check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_2_var = 6 }
					check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_3_var = 6 }
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				or = {
					check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^6 = 36 }
					check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^6 = 39 }
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			set_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_3_var = 6 }
		}
		100 = {
			modifier = {
				or = {
					check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_1_var = 7 }
					check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_2_var = 7 }
					check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_3_var = 7 }
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				or = {
					check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 = 36 }
					check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 = 39 }
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			set_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_3_var = 7 }
		}
	}
	clear_variable = ROC_coin_selected_id_var
	clear_variable = ROC_coin_selected_var
	clear_variable = ROC_coin_selected_type_var
	set_variable = { coin_flipped_sound_var = 0 }
	clear_array = ROC_coin_flipped_id_array
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_1_var = 7 } }
		add_to_array = { ROC_coin_flipped_id_array = ROC_collection_coin_box^0 }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_array = { ROC_coin_flipped_id_array = ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_1_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_2_var = 7 } }
		add_to_array = { ROC_coin_flipped_id_array = ROC_collection_coin_box^0 }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_array = { ROC_coin_flipped_id_array = ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_2_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_3_var = 7 } }
		add_to_array = { ROC_coin_flipped_id_array = ROC_collection_coin_box^0 }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_array = { ROC_coin_flipped_id_array = ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_3_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_1_var = 7 } }
				check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 > 38 }
				not = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 > 43 } }
			}
			else = {
				check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_1_var > 38 }
				not = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_1_var > 43 } }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { coin_flipped_sound_var = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_2_var = 7 } }
				check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 > 38 }
				not = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 > 43 } }
			}
			else = {
				check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_2_var > 38 }
				not = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_2_var > 43 } }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { coin_flipped_sound_var = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_3_var = 7 } }
				check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 > 38 }
				not = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 > 43 } }
			}
			else = {
				check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_3_var > 38 }
				not = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_3_var > 43 } }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { coin_flipped_sound_var = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_1_var = 7 } }
				check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 > 43 }
				not = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 > 49 } }
			}
			else = {
				check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_1_var > 43 }
				not = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_1_var > 49 } }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { coin_flipped_sound_var = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_2_var = 7 } }
				check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 > 43 }
				not = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 > 49 } }
			}
			else = {
				check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_2_var > 43 }
				not = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_2_var > 49 } }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { coin_flipped_sound_var = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_3_var = 7 } }
				check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 > 43 }
				not = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^0 > 49 } }
			}
			else = {
				check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_3_var > 43 }
				not = { check_variable = { ROC_collection_coin_box^ROC_coin_flipped_3_var > 49 } }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { coin_flipped_sound_var = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { coin_flipped_sound_var = 3 } }
		scoped_sound_effect = "hua_coin_get"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { coin_flipped_sound_var = -3 } }
		scoped_sound_effect = "li_coin_get"
	}
	else = {
		scoped_sound_effect = "heng_coin_get"
	}
	ROC_coin_flipped_idea_effect = yes
	set_country_flag = show_ROC_coin_flipped_window
	set_variable = { ROC_coin_flipped_auto_countdown_days = 180 }
}

ROC_Collection_sort_effect = {  ##
	clear_array = KMT_collection_unusing_array_temp
	for_each_loop = {
		array = KMT_collection_unusing_array
		value = v
		break = break
		add_to_array = { KMT_collection_unusing_array_temp = v }
	}
	clear_array = KMT_collection_unusing_array
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			any_of = {
				array = KMT_collection_unusing_array_temp
				value = v
				index = i
				check_variable = { v < 999 }
			}
		}
		find_lowest_in_array = {
			array = KMT_collection_unusing_array_temp
			value = temp_lowest_v
			index = temp_i
		}
		add_to_array = { KMT_collection_unusing_array = temp_lowest_v }
		set_variable = { KMT_collection_unusing_array_temp^temp_i = 999 }
	}
}


#五年计划
ROC_five_year_plan_completed_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				ROC_five_year_plan_completed_num < 25
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROC_five_year_plan_completed_num = 1
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = ROC_five_year_plan_over_tt
	}
}

ROC_army_modifier_tt = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_NRA_modifier } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = ROC_change_NRA_dynamic_modifier
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_Yan_NRA_modifier } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = ROC_change_Yan_NRA_modifier
	}
}

# 读当前槽：解出当月天数与月码（每月只跑一次）
ROC_lunar_read_slot = {
	set_variable = { ROC_lunar_mkey = ROC_lunar_tbl^ROC_lunar_pos }
	set_variable = { ROC_lunar_mlen = 29 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_lunar_mkey > 100 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { ROC_lunar_mkey = 100 }
		set_variable = { ROC_lunar_mlen = 30 }
	}
}

# 每天：日加一，跨月才进位到下一槽
ROC_lunar_daily_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { ROC_lunar_day = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ROC_lunar_day > ROC_lunar_mlen } }
		set_variable = { ROC_lunar_day = 1 }
		add_to_variable = { ROC_lunar_pos = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { ROC_lunar_tbl^ROC_lunar_pos = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { ROC_lunar_pos = 1 }
		}
		ROC_lunar_read_slot = yes
		#农历正月初一 = 农历新年
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { ROC_lunar_mkey = 1 } }
			#赤霄纪年已开始就先 +1（农历年，正月初一换年）
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = ROC_chixiao_era
					check_variable = { ROC_chixiao_era > 0 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROC_chixiao_era = 1 }
			}
			#统一后次年农历新年（第 1 个正月初一）：赤霄元年
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = ROC_reunit_lunar_years }
				add_to_variable = { ROC_reunit_lunar_years = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_reunit_lunar_years < 1 } } }
					set_variable = { ROC_chixiao_era = 1 }
					clear_variable = ROC_reunit_lunar_years
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_leader = {
								character = ROC_Chen
								ruling_only = yes
							}
						}
						set_country_flag = use_era_name_ROC
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# ========================================================
# 蒋巾帼右线：新生活社会改造总览
# ========================================================
ROC_social_reform_init_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ROC_social_reform_init } }
		set_country_flag = ROC_social_reform_init
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_youth_force = 35 }
		# 四界初始旧病灶（0~100，越低越好）
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_rural_disease = 75 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_youth_disease = 65 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_business_disease = 80 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_military_disease = 70 }
		# 四界初始改造度（0~上限，越高越好）
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_rural_reform = 15 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_youth_reform = 20 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_business_reform = 10 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_military_reform = 15 }
	}
	ROC_social_reform_recalc_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_social_reform_modifier } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_social_reform_modifier }
	}
}

ROC_social_reform_recalc_effect = {
	# 计算各领域改造上限（未破局时受制于旧势力，完成对应破局国策后解锁至100）
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = ROC_focus_crackdown_on_despotic_landlords
				has_country_flag = ROC_sr_rural_cap_unlocked
			}
		}
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_rural_cap = 100 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_rural_cap = 65 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = ROC_focus_paramilitary_youth_corps
				has_country_flag = ROC_sr_youth_cap_unlocked
			}
		}
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_youth_cap = 100 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_youth_cap = 70 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = ROC_focus_swat_flies_and_strike_tigers
				has_country_flag = ROC_sr_business_cap_unlocked
			}
		}
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_business_cap = 100 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_business_cap = 60 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = ROC_focus_crackdown_on_military_factions
				has_country_flag = ROC_sr_military_cap_unlocked
			}
		}
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_military_cap = 100 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_military_cap = 65 }
	}

	# 钳制数值范围
	clamp_variable = { var = ROC_sr_youth_force min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = ROC_sr_rural_disease min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = ROC_sr_youth_disease min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = ROC_sr_business_disease min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = ROC_sr_military_disease min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = ROC_sr_rural_reform min = 0 max = ROC_sr_rural_cap }
	clamp_variable = { var = ROC_sr_youth_reform min = 0 max = ROC_sr_youth_cap }
	clamp_variable = { var = ROC_sr_business_reform min = 0 max = ROC_sr_business_cap }
	clamp_variable = { var = ROC_sr_military_reform min = 0 max = ROC_sr_military_cap }

	# 计算全境均值
	set_temp_variable = { tmp_disease_sum = ROC_sr_rural_disease }
	add_to_temp_variable = { tmp_disease_sum = ROC_sr_youth_disease }
	add_to_temp_variable = { tmp_disease_sum = ROC_sr_business_disease }
	add_to_temp_variable = { tmp_disease_sum = ROC_sr_military_disease }
	divide_temp_variable = { tmp_disease_sum = 4 }
	set_variable = { ROC_sr_avg_disease = tmp_disease_sum }

	set_temp_variable = { tmp_reform_sum = ROC_sr_rural_reform }
	add_to_temp_variable = { tmp_reform_sum = ROC_sr_youth_reform }
	add_to_temp_variable = { tmp_reform_sum = ROC_sr_business_reform }
	add_to_temp_variable = { tmp_reform_sum = ROC_sr_military_reform }
	divide_temp_variable = { tmp_reform_sum = 4 }
	set_variable = { ROC_sr_avg_reform = tmp_reform_sum }

	# 黄金纪元达标判定：四界改造度均 >= 80 且 旧病灶均 <= 25
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_sr_rural_reform < 80 } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_sr_youth_reform < 80 } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_sr_business_reform < 80 } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_sr_military_reform < 80 } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_sr_rural_disease > 25 } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_sr_youth_disease > 25 } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_sr_business_disease > 25 } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_sr_military_disease > 25 } }
		}
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_golden_ready = 1 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_max_eff = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_army_attack = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_army_defence = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_mobilization = 0.15 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_compliance = 0.10 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_golden_ready = 0 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_max_eff = 0 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_army_attack = 0 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_army_defence = 0 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_mobilization = 0 }
		set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_compliance = 0 }
	}

	# 过程动态增益：随平均改造度 0%~100% 线性成长，满额各 +10%
	set_temp_variable = { tmp_sr_scale = ROC_sr_avg_reform }
	multiply_temp_variable = { tmp_sr_scale = 0.001 }
	set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_buildings_speed = tmp_sr_scale }
	set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_factory_output = tmp_sr_scale }
	set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_pp_factor = tmp_sr_scale }
	set_variable = { ROC_sr_mod_stability = tmp_sr_scale }
}

# 1. 乡村基层：柔和妥协 / 铁腕清洗
ROC_sr_rural_soft_effect = {
	add_political_power = -25
	add_stability = 0.01
	add_to_variable = { ROC_sr_youth_force = 5 }
	add_to_variable = { ROC_sr_rural_disease = -4 }
	add_to_variable = { ROC_sr_rural_reform = 8 }
	ROC_social_reform_recalc_effect = yes
}
ROC_sr_rural_hard_effect = {
	add_to_variable = { ROC_sr_youth_force = -15 }
	add_stability = -0.02
	add_to_variable = { ROC_sr_rural_disease = -14 }
	add_to_variable = { ROC_sr_rural_reform = 12 }
	ROC_social_reform_recalc_effect = yes
}

# ========================================================
# 黄埔线：训政中枢办公桌（一底三面 · 副手机制联动）
# ========================================================
ROC_whampoa_cd_sync_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ROC_wt_btn_1_cd }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ROC_wt_btn_1_cd_days } }
			set_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_1_cd_days = 30 }
		}
		set_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_1_cd_pct = ROC_wt_btn_1_cd_days }
		multiply_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_1_cd_pct = 100 }
		divide_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_1_cd_pct = 60 }
		round_variable = ROC_wt_btn_1_cd_pct
		clamp_variable = { var = ROC_wt_btn_1_cd_pct min = 2 max = 100 }
	}
	else = {
		clear_variable = ROC_wt_btn_1_cd_days
		clear_variable = ROC_wt_btn_1_cd_pct
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ROC_wt_btn_2_cd }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ROC_wt_btn_2_cd_days } }
			set_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_2_cd_days = 30 }
		}
		set_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_2_cd_pct = ROC_wt_btn_2_cd_days }
		multiply_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_2_cd_pct = 100 }
		divide_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_2_cd_pct = 60 }
		round_variable = ROC_wt_btn_2_cd_pct
		clamp_variable = { var = ROC_wt_btn_2_cd_pct min = 2 max = 100 }
	}
	else = {
		clear_variable = ROC_wt_btn_2_cd_days
		clear_variable = ROC_wt_btn_2_cd_pct
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ROC_wt_btn_3_cd }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ROC_wt_btn_3_cd_days } }
			set_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_3_cd_days = 30 }
		}
		set_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_3_cd_pct = ROC_wt_btn_3_cd_days }
		multiply_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_3_cd_pct = 100 }
		divide_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_3_cd_pct = 60 }
		round_variable = ROC_wt_btn_3_cd_pct
		clamp_variable = { var = ROC_wt_btn_3_cd_pct min = 2 max = 100 }
	}
	else = {
		clear_variable = ROC_wt_btn_3_cd_days
		clear_variable = ROC_wt_btn_3_cd_pct
	}
}

ROC_whampoa_tutelage_init_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ROC_whampoa_init } }
		set_country_flag = ROC_whampoa_init
		set_variable = { ROC_wt_hu_m1 = 15 }
		set_variable = { ROC_wt_hu_m2 = 0 }
		set_variable = { ROC_wt_tang_m1 = 15 }
		set_variable = { ROC_wt_tang_m2 = 0 }
		set_variable = { ROC_wt_hos_m1 = 15 }
		set_variable = { ROC_wt_hos_m2 = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_whampoa_tutelage_modifier } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ROC_whampoa_tutelage_modifier }
	}
	ROC_whampoa_tutelage_recalc_effect = yes
}

ROC_whampoa_tutelage_recalc_effect = {

	# 1. 上半区双轨上限判定：初始 50 -> 《民主必要之训政》后 75 -> 中轴解锁至 100
	set_temp_variable = { tmp_wt_base_cap = 50 }

	set_variable = { ROC_wt_tang_m1_cap = tmp_wt_base_cap }

		limit = { has_completed_focus = ROC_focus_old_hus_testament }

						NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_wt_hos_m1 < 80 } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_wt_hos_m2 < 80 } }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROC_wt_deputy_tier = 2 }
				add_to_variable = { ROC_wt_mod_factory_output = 0.10 }
				add_to_variable = { ROC_wt_mod_build_speed = 0.10 }
				add_to_variable = { ROC_wt_mod_consumer_goods = -0.05 }
				add_to_variable = { ROC_wt_mod_max_eff = 0.05 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_wt_hos_m1 < 50 } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { ROC_wt_hos_m2 < 50 } }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROC_wt_deputy_tier = 1 }
				add_to_variable = { ROC_wt_mod_consumer_goods = -0.03 }
				add_to_variable = { ROC_wt_mod_build_speed = 0.05 }
			}
		}
	}
	ROC_whampoa_cd_sync_effect = yes
}

# 每日按钮冷却计时同步（由 on_daily_ROC 调用）
ROC_whampoa_daily_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ROC_wt_btn_1_cd
			has_variable = ROC_wt_btn_1_cd_days
			check_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_1_cd_days > 1 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_1_cd_days = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ROC_wt_btn_2_cd
			has_variable = ROC_wt_btn_2_cd_days
			check_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_2_cd_days > 1 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_2_cd_days = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ROC_wt_btn_3_cd
			has_variable = ROC_wt_btn_3_cd_days
			check_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_3_cd_days > 1 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { ROC_wt_btn_3_cd_days = 1 }
	}
	ROC_whampoa_cd_sync_effect = yes
}

# 月度副手巡航脉冲（由 ROC_monthly_check_effect 调用）
ROC_whampoa_monthly_effect = {
	ROC_whampoa_tutelage_recalc_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_leader = {
				character = ROC_Hu_Zongnan
				ruling_only = yes
			}
			has_idea = ROC_Dai_Jitao_idea
		}
		add_to_variable = { ROC_wt_hu_m2 = 2 }
		add_to_variable = { ROC_wt_hu_m2 = ROC_wt_ra_lv }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_leader = {
				character = ROC_Tangtang
				ruling_only = yes
			}
			has_idea = ROC_Mi_Fu_idea
		}
		add_to_variable = { ROC_wt_tang_m1 = 2 }
		add_to_variable = { ROC_wt_tang_m2 = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_leader = {
				character = ROC_Herrscher_of_Sentience
				ruling_only = yes
			}
			has_idea = ROC_Sun_Ke_idea
		}
		add_to_variable = { ROC_wt_hos_m2 = 3 }
	}
	ROC_whampoa_tutelage_recalc_effect = yes
}

# 将附庸国 FROM 的子意识形态转化为与宗主国 ROOT 相同
ROC_sync_FROM_subideology_to_ROOT_effect = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = ROC_sync_FROM_subideology_to_ROOT_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { FROM = { has_government = ROOT } } }
			if = { limit = { ROOT = { has_government = communism } } FROM = { set_politics = { ruling_party = communism } } }
			else_if = { limit = { ROOT = { has_government = democratic } } FROM = { set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic } } }
			else_if = { limit = { ROOT = { has_government = neutrality } } FROM = { set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality } } }
			else_if = { limit = { ROOT = { has_government = fascism } } FROM = { set_politics = { ruling_party = fascism } } }
		}
		if = { limit = { ROOT = { has_country_leader_ideology = Three_Principles_of_the_People_c } } FROM = { set_country_leader_ideology = Three_Principles_of_the_People_c } }
		else_if = { limit = { ROOT = { has_country_leader_ideology = Monarchical_socialism } } FROM = { set_country_leader_ideology = Monarchical_socialism } }
